This website is incredibly helpful! I am traveling to Bordeaux the end of June and we are flying into Orly; what would you recommend as the best way to get to the Montparnsse Station; metro or taxi?\n\n\n\n\n\n    [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-20980)\n\n    - Hi! The metro stations have a lot of stairs, which can be a real hassle when you’re carrying luggage. Escalators and elevators are not available in every station. Honestly, a taxi or Uber is worth it when dealing with luggage.\n\n\n\n\n\n      [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-21253)\n24. Hoping you can help as all the above information is a bit overwhelming. On Sept 18 we need to travel from Bordeaux to Munich. It seems that the best option is to take the train to Paris then fly Lufthansa from Paris to Munich.\n\n\n\n    I’m trying to figure out the train and metro schedules to determine if we can make a 2:30p flight out of CDG to Munich. Can you please help me?\n\n\n\n\n\n    [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-21163)\n\n    - To go the distance from Bordeaux to Munich, it is best to fly. You can fly from Bordeaux to Munich. Even with the connection, which is usually in Amsterdam via KLM, it will be far less expensive, faster and remove all the stress of getting to Paris to transfer to the airport for a flight.\n\n\n\n\n\n      [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-21252)\n25. Great information on this site. Much appreciated.\n\n\n    I will be traveling by train from Paris Montparnasse to Bordeaux and then changing trains with Angouleme as my final destination next month. Although I have a coach and seat number on the Paris to Bordeaux train, all I have is a train number and barcode for the Bordeaux to Angouleme ride.\n\n\n    My ticket was booked through RailEurope. Does no coach and seat number mean that I can take any coach and seat on the ride to Angouleme?\n\n\n    I look forward to your reply. Thank you in advance.\n\n\n\n\n\n    [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-21773)\n\n    - The OUIGO train between Bordeaux and Paris often already makes a stop already in Angouleme. I’m a bit confused. Are you staying in Bordeaux to visit and then going to Angouleme? Otherwise, Angouleme is in between Paris and Bordeaux, so there would be no reason to go all the way to Bordeaux to then go back north to Angouleme.\n\n\n\n\n\n      [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-22371)\n\n      - Hi Jennifer. My final destination is Angouleme. Apparently there is no direct train from Paris to Angouleme on the day of my travel (5/18/24). So I have to go to Bordeaux and then change trains and go from Bordeaux to Angouleme. I have about 10 minutes to make that transfer. And there is no coach or seat number on my ticket. Can you offer any advice on a change of trains in Bordeaux? And because I do not have a seat number, can I assume that I can sit anywhere? Thanks again.\n\n\n\n\n\n        [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-22401)\n\n        - There are a lot of French holidays in May and that is a Saturday, so the direct trains are likely already sold out. I had a look and presumably you have one of the trains booked that changes in Bordeaux. You change to a TER train, which is a regional train. These don’t have assigned seats. So when you change, you just get on and find an empty seat. And don’t worry about the amount of time to change – 10 minutes is plenty of time! I’ve even taken my dog out to potty quickly with 10 minutes time. Gare Bordeaux Saint Jean is actually quite a compact train station.\n\n\n\n          What I advise is about 10 minutes before you are due to arrive in Bordeaux, gather your things and go to the door of the car to be ready to hop right off. You can just stand in the entrance area of the car, and you won’t be alone doing it. You can also look on the app or the website for Gare Bordeaux Saint Jean [https://www.garesetconnexions.sncf/fr/gares-services/bordeaux-saint-jean/horaires](https://www.garesetconnexions.sncf/fr/gares-services/bordeaux-saint-jean/horaires) ahead of your arrival to see which track your connecting train is on by clicking the Horaires tab. It will show you arriving and departing trains like an airport arrivals and departures board. There are also screens all over the station – on the tracks and in the hall connecting the tracks – which you can look at to see which track your train is on.\n\n\n\n          Hope that all helps!\n\n\n\n\n\n          [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-22411)\n\n          - That is such a great help Jennifer. Thank you so much!!\n26. Hi, to take the train from CDG to Bordeaux, which train station should we use at CDG? There appears to be 2. If our flights don’t line up, is there a train from CDG to Montparnasse? I saw a reference to a taxi. Thanks\n\n\n\n\n\n    [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-22288)\n\n    - There are 2 stops at CDG, one for Terminal 1 and one for Terminal 3. The same train lines run from both stops, so it only matters which Terminal you will be in. There is not a train from CDG to Montparnasse. You can take the RER and metro to reach Montparnasse or a taxi.\n\n\n\n\n\n      [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-22370)\n27. This is such a great website, thank you! I have a timing question about getting from CDG to Montparnasse. Traveling with family including our mother who is in her early 80s so needs to be leisurely paced. We arrive CDG around 12:35 on a Friday (August) and there is a train leaving Montparnasse at 15:11 but worried that will be a little tight since we are unfamiliar with everything, or possible flight delays. Thinking we would taxi/uber to Montparnasse. There are not many tickets left so hoping to book soon. Otherwise there are later options around 16:06 on a longer train, and 17:00 again a short one but then we have a long delay. Suggestions? Also, is it much cheaper to buy round trip since we are still figuring out our return plans, wondering if we could just get teh outbound now or if it will cost a lot more. Thank you!\n\n\n\n\n\n    [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-23131)\n\n    - Hi Michelle,\n\n\n\n      In a best case scenario of your flight arriving on time, planning around 1 hour for passport control and collecting your baggage, plus the travel time by taxi to Montparnasse, you’ll arrive within 2 hours of landing at CDG. So I feel like a train at 15:11 is very much cutting it close and there is a real possibility you would miss the train. I would opt for the 17:00 train to give you some cushion of time, and I honestly think you will be waiting to board for a much shorter amount of time than you think. There are plenty of restaurants, cafes and shops in Montparnasse. The station is almost like a shopping mall.\n\n\n\n      Also, train tickets are sold by one way. Buying round trip doesn’t make the fares any cheaper. But as August and September are pretty much the peak travel times – and even more so this year with the Olympics and Paralympics during that period – I would suggest purchasing your train tickets as soon as possible once you figure out your return plans.\n\n\n\n\n\n      [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-23228)\n28. Hello! Great blog – thank you. Forgive the slight deviation, but would you be able to advise if an hour and 5 minutes would be sufficient to get from Gare du Nord to Montparnasse? We’ll be coming from the UK and hoping to get the SCNF from Montparnasse to Bordeaux – although I can’t see many options on Trainline/Eurostar with a longer transfer time.\n\n\n\n    Many thanks!\n\n\n    Sakib\n\n\n\n\n\n    [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-23351)\n\n    - Yes, you should be fine! From Gare du Nord, you can take the M4 line to Montparnasse. It should only take 20 minutes as it’s a direct metro line between the stations. Just don’t waste any time in between. I’d also book the flex ticket that allows you to make changes in case of any delays on your Eurostar train.\n\n\n\n\n\n      [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-23492)\n\n      - Amazing, thank you!\n\n\n\n\n\n        [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-23527)\n29. Hi Jennifer, and thank you so much for the excellent information!\n\n\n    I will be flying to Paris in May 2025 and, after a few days stay, taking a train from Paris to Bordeaux. I’ll be bringing along a bicycle, which will be packed in a large cardboard box. What options exist to get 2 travelers with luggage and 2 large bicycle boxes from Paris to Bordeaux?\n\n\n    Thank you in advance for any help you can provide!\n\n\n\n\n\n    [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-40182)\n\n    - Hi, you can take a bagged and dismantled/foldable bike on this route. There’s an option to add the bike when you make your ticket reservation and currently the fee is €5. My husband has taken his boxed up bicycle on the train when he flew from the US. What he did was cut a “handle” in the box and attached a small flat dolly cart with some tape. Don’t check the bicycle box in with the dolly attached. Put the dolly and tape in your carry on, and attach it after you pick up the bicycle box from baggage claim. He also highly recommends attaching an Apple Air Tag to your bicycle inside the bike box so you can track it if the airline loses your luggage. Hope that helps!\n\n\n\n\n\n      [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-41467)\n30. Thanks for this detailed summary, Jennifer. We’ll be in Dordogne for 10 days in June then taking TGV from Bordeaux to Paris for 4 days. We bought the tickets weeks ago through Rail Europe and have a QR code PDF ticket for each person. Do we just show/scan that code somewhere to access the train in Bordeaux? Or is there some process where we need to get a physical ticket at the station?\n\n\n\n    Also, any suggestions on wine tour/tasting in the Dordogne region? We’re staying in Le Bugue but will have a rental car so an hour or so drive isn’t a big deal.\n\n\n\n    Thanks!\n\n\n\n\n\n    [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-86206)\n\n    - Your QR code is your ticket. In Bordeaux, sometimes you have controllers scanning tickets as you enter the platform and you cannot pass the gate to the platform without them scanning your ticket. Other times the controllers come through the train and scan your ticket sometime during the journey. It just depends.\n\n\n\n      As for Dordogne wine, you have the majority of wineries around Bergerac and will be around 1 hour or less from where you are staying. Here are some places you can visit: [https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/discover-bergerac-wines/](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/discover-bergerac-wines/)\n\n\n\n      Enjoy your trip!\n\n\n\n\n\n      [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-86449)\n31. Thanks for all your great info about the trains between Paris and Bordeaux. Are you aware if a US passport or drivers license is sufficient to show someone qualifies for the “senior” train ticket rate? I’ve seen references to needing to have a special identification for this. Thank you!\n\n\n\n\n\n    [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-111166)\n\n    - To get a senior discount advantage, you have to purchase a card called Carte Advantage Senior for the price of €49. The advantage card is good for 1 year and gives card holders 30% off all bookings. You would have to assess how much you will actually use the train for your trip and if the 30% savings is more than the €49 cost of the card. You can order the card online and receive it digitally here: [https://www.sncf-connect.com/catalogue/description/carte-avantage-senior](https://www.sncf-connect.com/catalogue/description/carte-avantage-senior)?\n\n\n\n\n\n      [Reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/#comment-129747)\n\n### Leave a Reply [Cancel reply](https://bordeauxtravelguide.com/paris-to-bordeaux-train/?fbclid=IwAR2jaFmof0Fz9Yko48AnZ_Hxvug3ncGLpL9jG9IDQETfnAh6_251_lBL99E\\#respond)\n\nYour email address will not be published.Required fields are marked \\*\n\nComment \\*\n\nName \\*\n\nEmail \\*\n\nSave my name, email, and website in this browser for the next time I comment.\n\nNotify me of follow-up comments by email.\n\nNotify me of new posts by email.\n\nΔ\n\nThis site uses Akismet to reduce spam. [Learn how your comment data is processed.](https://akismet.com/privacy/)",

Authorize cookies to access to this feature.\n[Allow](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#) [Personalizing](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)**Addtoany** is disabled. Authorize cookies to access to this feature.\n[Allow](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#) [Personalizing](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n- [Share on Linkedin (new window)](https://www.ratp.fr/#linkedin) **Linkedin** is disabled. Authorize cookies to access to this feature.\n[Allow](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#) [Personalizing](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)**Addtoany** is disabled. Authorize cookies to access to this feature.\n[Allow](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#) [Personalizing](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n\n# Bus-Tram tickets\nIf you wish to travel by bus, tram or Noctilien in the Île-de-France region, then the Bus-Tram ticket is what you need!\n\n**€2,05***\n\n*full fare\n\n[See fares](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#2-attached-to-anchor)\n\n![](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/styles/card_m/public/2025-08/Ticket_0.png)\n\nLast modified on 26 May 2026 at 11h21\n\n## How does it work?\nWith the Bus-Tram ticket, you can travel across the overground networks in the Île-de-France region.\n\nThis ticket is valid on bus, tram, Noctilien, Filéo, Tzen and C1 lines within a 90-minute limit. While connections are allowed, journeys cannot be interrupted and resumed on the same line, and return trips are not allowed.\n\nThe ticket can be loaded onto a Navigo Easy pass, on your smart phone or smart watch.\n\n![](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/inline-images/comment_ca_marche_6.png)\n\n## How much does it cost?\n- Children from 4 to under 10 years old.\n- Solidarité Transport pass holders.\n- Holders of a \"Familles nombreuses\" card bearing the mention \"valid in Île-de-France\" (for more information, please visit [[https://www.carte-familles-nombreuses.gouv.fr/home](https://www.carte-familles-nombreuses.gouv.fr/home)](https://www.carte-familles-nombreuses.gouv.fr/home));\n- People with a certified disability/French CMI (carte mobilité inclusion, mobility inclusiveness card) cardholders.\n- ONAC (Office national des combattants et des victimes de guerre, French national veteran and war victim body) cardholders (single blue stripe).\n- People accompanying passengers with a certified disability.\n- Groups of 10 or more young people under the age of 16 and their chaperone*, or middle and high-school students (including those in the final year) who are travelling in groups of 10 and are accompanied by a teacher* are eligible. Chaperones must produce a duly completed signed [statement](https://portail-idfm.cdn.prismic.io/portail-idfm/aNv-Bp5xUNkB1SYr_D%C3%A9clarationGroupedejeunes-enfantsIDFM2025.pdf)\n- Groups of at least 8 children and their chaperone travelling as part of a school outing or extra-curricular trip organised by a primary school or a primary school recreational group. Chaperones must produce a duly completed signed [statement](https://portail-idfm.cdn.prismic.io/portail-idfm/aNv-Bp5xUNkB1SYr_D%C3%A9clarationGroupedejeunes-enfantsIDFM2025.pdf)\n\n## Where to buy it?\n[In our stations](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n\n[Online](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n\n_*Or official Île-de-France Mobilités distributor apps._ _App contracted by Île-de-France Mobilités to distribute digital versions of public transport tickets across the Île-de-France regional network._ _Find the full list of official distributors at_ [_[www.iledefrance-mobilites.fr](http://www.iledefrance-mobilites.fr)_](https://www.iledefrance-mobilites.fr/en/revendeurs-officiels)\n\n_**The Bonjour RATP app is developed by RATP Smart Systems._\n\n[Outside RATP stations](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n\n- From [commerces agréés RATP](https://www.ratp.fr/titres-et-tarifs/les-points-de-vente/commerces-proximite)\n\n![](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/inline-images/Ou%20acheter%20souscrire_15.png)\n\n## General conditions of use\n[Consult the Terms and Conditions of use](https://www.iledefrance-mobilites.fr/cgvu)",

Authorize cookies to access to this feature.\n[Allow](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#) [Personalizing](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)**Addtoany** is disabled. Authorize cookies to access to this feature.\n[Allow](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#) [Personalizing](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n\n[Print / Download this content(Open in new window)](https://www.ratp.fr/printable/print/node/35940)\n\n# Bus-Tram tickets\n\n- ![Busratp](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/lines-assets/picto/busratp/symbole.1634824971.svg)\n- ![Tram](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/lines-assets/picto/tram/symbole.1634824971.svg)\n- ![Cable](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/lines-assets/picto/cable/symbole.1765961602.svg)\n\nIf you wish to travel by bus, tram or Noctilien in the Île-de-France region, then the Bus-Tram ticket is what you need!\n\n\n**€2,05\\***\n\n\\*full fare\n\n\n[See fares](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#2-attached-to-anchor)\n\n![](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/styles/card_m/public/2025-08/Ticket_0.png)\n\nLast modified on 26 May 2026 at 11h21\n\n\n## Summary\n\n1. [How does it work?](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#1-attached-to-anchor)\n2. [How much does it cost?](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#2-attached-to-anchor)\n3. [Where to buy it?](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#3-attached-to-anchor)\n4. [General conditions of use](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#4-attached-to-anchor)\n5. [Frequently asked questions](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#5-attached-to-anchor)\n\n## How does it work?\n\nWith the Bus-Tram ticket, you can travel across the overground networks in the Île-de-France region.\n\nThis ticket is valid on bus, tram, Noctilien, Filéo, Tzen and C1 lines within a 90-minute limit. While connections are allowed, journeys cannot be interrupted and resumed on the same line, and return trips are not allowed.\n\nThe ticket can be loaded onto a Navigo Easy pass, on your smart phone or smart watch.\n\n![](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/inline-images/comment_ca_marche_6.png)\n\n## How much does it cost?\n\n![](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/inline-images/Combien%20ca%20coute_0.png)\n\n_Prices from January 1st 2026_\n\nThe Bus-Tram ticket has a flat fare, regardless of the distance travelled.\n\n|  | Full fare | Reduced fare |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Single ticket | €2,05 | €1,05 |\n\nDid you know that you can travel for less with a [Navigo Liberté +](https://www.ratp.fr/en/node/11794) travel pass?\n\n[Eligibility for reduced fares](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n\nThe following passengers benefit from a 50% discount on single Bus-Tram tickets:\n\n- Children from 4 to under 10 years old.\n- Solidarité Transport pass holders.\n- Holders of a \"Familles nombreuses\" card bearing the mention \"valid in Île-de-France\" (for more information, please visit [https://www.carte-familles-nombreuses.gouv.fr/home](https://www.carte-familles-nombreuses.gouv.fr/home));\n- People with a certified disability/French CMI (carte mobilité inclusion, mobility inclusiveness card) cardholders.\n- ONAC (Office national des combattants et des victimes de guerre, French national veteran and war victim body) cardholders (single blue stripe).\n- People accompanying passengers with a certified disability.\n- Groups of 10 or more young people under the age of 16 and their chaperone\\*, or middle and high-school students (including those in the final year) who are travelling in groups of 10 and are accompanied by a teacher\\* are eligible. Chaperones must produce a duly completed signed [statement](https://portail-idfm.cdn.prismic.io/portail-idfm/aNv-Bp5xUNkB1SYr_D%C3%A9clarationGroupedejeunes-enfantsIDFM2025.pdf)\n- Groups of at least 8 children and their chaperone travelling as part of a school outing or extra-curricular trip organised by a primary school or a primary school recreational group. Chaperones must produce a duly completed signed [statement](https://portail-idfm.cdn.prismic.io/portail-idfm/aNv-Bp5xUNkB1SYr_D%C3%A9clarationGroupedejeunes-enfantsIDFM2025.pdf)\n\n_\\\\* Teachers and chaperones are also entitled to half-price tickets. Larger groups may be accompanied by one chaperone for every 10 students._\n\n## Where to buy it?\n\n[In our stations](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n\n- Station staff at ticket desks in metro and RER stations\n- Ticket machines in our metro and RER stations\n\n[Online](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n\n- In the [IDF Mobilités](https://www.iledefrance-mobilites.fr/titres-et-tarifs/supports/smartphone) (\\*) and [Bonjour RATP](https://www.bonjour-ratp.fr/en/aide-contact/?question=how-do-i-buy-tickets-using-the-application) (\\*\\*) apps\n- Companies and institutions can purchase from the Île-de-France Mobilités corporate client page [Accueil Grands Comptes \\| Ile-de-France mobilités (iledefrance-mobilites.fr)](https://grands-comptes.iledefrance-mobilites.fr/)\n\n_\\*Or official Île-de-France Mobilités distributor apps. App contracted by Île-de-France Mobilités to distribute digital versions of public transport tickets across the Île-de-France regional network. Find the full list of official distributors at_ [_www.iledefrance-mobilites.fr_](https://www.iledefrance-mobilites.fr/en/revendeurs-officiels)\n\n_\\*\\*The Bonjour RATP app is developed by RATP Smart Systems._\n\n[Outside RATP stations](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#)\n\n- From [commerces agréés RATP](https://www.ratp.fr/titres-et-tarifs/les-points-de-vente/commerces-proximite)\n\n![](https://www.ratp.fr/sites/default/files/inline-images/Ou%20acheter%20souscrire_15.png)\n\n## General conditions of use\n\n[Consult the Terms and Conditions of use](https://www.iledefrance-mobilites.fr/cgvu)\n\n## Frequently asked questions\n\n[Help and contacts \\| Île-de-France Mobilités (iledefrance-mobilites.fr)](https://www.iledefrance-mobilites.fr/aide-et-contacts)\n\nnewrelic\\_footer\n\nCopy link\n\n✓\n\nThanks for sharing!\n\nFind any service\n\n[AddToAny](https://www.addtoany.com/ \"Share Buttons\")\n\n[More…](https://www.ratp.fr/en/titres-et-tarifs/ticket-bus-tram#addtoany \"Show all\")\n\nA2A",

        "description": "# Trains, bus, public transport: easily find your journey\n## Find your perfect city break:\n[![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_max_width_961/public/media/2026-01/bons-plans-hp-lt_302x200_londres.jpg?itok=fqouN5pt)\\ \\ Eurostar\\ \\ Fancy Europe? \\ \\ **Fancy Europe?** **Eurostar**\\ \\ Take advantage of fares from €29, whether booking at short notice or planning further ahead, for travel up to 5 September 2027 with Eurostar. \\ Travel to London, Brussels, Amsterdam, Cologne and many other cities …\\ (2)\\ \\\n\n- \n  - *\\\n\\\nBook now]([https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/deals/eurostar-sales-opening](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/deals/eurostar-sales-opening))\n\n- \n  - *\\\n\\\nDiscover]([https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/deals/ete-indien-eu-2026](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/deals/ete-indien-eu-2026))\n\n[![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_max_width_961/public/media/2017-08/23/palais-carmen-steiner-westend61-photononstop-iob.jpg?itok=ei9hvhq1)\\ \\ **Avignon the city of Provence!** \\ \\ We love Avignon for its cultural richness and its specialties with the scent of Provence, but the city of the Popes offers many other surprises! \\ \\\n\n- \n  - *\\\n\\\nLet’s go]([https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/train-ticket?vscTrainTravel=oneway&vscTrainOrigin=Paris&vscTrainOriginRR=FRPAR&vscTrainDestination=Avignon_tgv&vscTrainDestinationRR=FRAVG&comfortClass=2&directTravel=true](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/train-ticket?vscTrainTravel=oneway&vscTrainOrigin=Paris&vscTrainOriginRR=FRPAR&vscTrainDestination=Avignon_tgv&vscTrainDestinationRR=FRAVG&comfortClass=2&directTravel=true))\n\n[![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_max_width_961/public/media/2020-09/300X300-lyon.jpg?itok=TpVMpDsx)\\ \\ A gourmet getaway\\ \\ Lyon, where culture meets gastronomy\\ \\ **Lyon, where culture meets gastronomy A gourmet getaway**\\ \\ Take advantage of departures throughout the day to explore the city of Lyon: stroll along the riverbanks, enjoy unmissable culinary delights, and discover the traboules of Vieux Lyon and Croix-Rousse. \\ (3)\\ \\\n\n![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/image_scale_width_750/public/media/2026-10/option_master_750x490_en_0.jpg?itok=s_Pd1iFJ)\n\n![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/image_scale_width_750/public/media/2026-10/option_master_750x490_en_0.jpg?itok=s_Pd1iFJ)\n\n## One app to manage all your trips\nWith one touch, you can organise, book and manage your day-to-day trips as well as long-distance trips.\n\n## Legal mentions\n### (1) All abroad for the Disney Halloween Festival!\n(1) ***Adult fares:** Price including VAT from €19 for a one-way journey with OUIGO, on selected days and trains, to a selection of more than 60 destinations in France and Belgium, subject to availability of seats at this fare on eligible trains. Offer valid for travel from 4 July to 12 December 2026 inclusive. Booking required no later than 10 minutes before train departure. Tickets are non-refundable and exchangeable subject to conditions. This offer cannot be combined with any other current promotion. Sales and information available on OUIGO.com, via the OUIGO app or from SNCF-approved online travel agencies.\n\n**List of destinations:** Aéroport Roissy Charles de Gaulle TGV ; Agde ; Agen ; Aix en Provence TGV ; Angers Saint-Laud ; Angoulême ; Antibes ; Aulnoye-Aymeries ; Auray ; Avignon TGV ; Béziers ; Bordeaux Saint-Jean ; Brest ; Bruxelles-Midi ; Cannes ; Creil ; Guingamp ; La Rochelle ; Laval ; Le Mans ; Les Arcs-Draguignan ; Lille ; Lorient ; Lyon (Lyon Perrache, Lyon Part Dieu, Lyon Saint Exupéry Aéroport) ; Marne-la-Vallée Chessy ; Marseille Saint-Charles ; Massy TGV ; Metz (Gare Lorraine TGV) ; Montauban ; Montpellier (Montpellier Saint-Roch, Montpellier Sud de France) ; Mons ; Morlaix ; Nantes ; Narbonne ; Nice ; Nîmes ; Niort ; Paris (Paris Est, Paris Gare de Lyon, Paris Gare du Nord, Paris Montparnasse) ; Perpignan ; Poitiers ; Quimper ; Rennes ; Saint-Raphaël Valescure ; Saint-Brieuc ; Saint-Quentin ; Sète ; Strasbourg ; Surgères ; TGV Haute Picardie ; Toulon ; Toulouse ; Tourcoing ; Tours (Gare St Pierre des Corps) ; Valence TGV Rhône Alpes Sud ; Vannes.",

        "markdown": "[Go to the main content](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/#main)\n\n# Trains, bus, public transport: easily find your journey\n\nSearch allTrainsBus and carpoolingUrban routes\n\nEnter a destination, a route…\n\nSearch\n\nValidate with the “Enter“ key\n\n- [Car rental](https://secure.location-voiture.sncf-connect.com/en/?cor=BE&cid=pcenen-sncf-057)\n- [Hotels](https://all.sncf-connect.com/home?lang=en&utm_source=sncf%20connect&utm_medium=HPMENUSECONDAIREdesk&utm_campaign=display#/)\n\n[![All abroad for the Disney Halloween Festival!](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/media/2026-10/26-10-partenariat-disney-halloween-hp_en_0.jpg)\\\\\n\\\\\n**All abroad for the Disney Halloween Festival!** \\\\\n\\\\\nBook your train to the Disney® Parks now!\\\\\n\\\\\nFrom\\\\\n\\\\\n19\\\\\n\\\\\n€(1)\\\\\n\\\\\n/one-way\\\\\n\\\\\nI shiver](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/deals/disneyland-paris)\n\n## Find your perfect city break:\n\n[![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_max_width_961/public/media/2026-01/bons-plans-hp-lt_302x200_londres.jpg?itok=fqouN5pt)\\\\\n\\\\\nEurostar\\\\\n\\\\\nFancy Europe? \\\\\n\\\\\n**Fancy Europe? Find your destination. It’s up to you!**\\\\\n\\\\\nClick on what you feel like doing and find your next destination.\\\\\n\\\\\n* * *\\\\\n\\\\\nDiscover](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/deals/ete-indien-eu-2026)\n\n* * *\n\n[![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_max_width_961/public/media/2017-08/23/palais-carmen-steiner-westend61-photononstop-iob.jpg?itok=ei9hvhq1)\\\\\n\\\\\n**Avignon the city of Provence!**\\\\\n\\\\\nWe love Avignon for its cultural richness and its specialties with the scent of Provence, but the city of the Popes offers many other surprises!\\\\\n\\\\\n* * *\\\\\n\\\\\nLet’s go](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/train-ticket?vscTrainTravel=oneway&vscTrainOrigin=Paris&vscTrainOriginRR=FRPAR&vscTrainDestination=Avignon_tgv&vscTrainDestinationRR=FRAVG&comfortClass=2&directTravel=true)\n\n* * *\n\n[![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_max_width_961/public/media/2020-09/300X300-lyon.jpg?itok=TpVMpDsx)\\\\\n\\\\\nA gourmet getaway\\\\\n\\\\\nLyon, where culture meets gastronomy\\\\\n\\\\\n**Lyon, where culture meets gastronomy A gourmet getaway**\\\\\n\\\\\nTake advantage of departures throughout the day to explore the city of Lyon: stroll along the riverbanks, enjoy unmissable culinary delights, and discover the traboules of Vieux Lyon and Croix-Rousse.\\\\\n(3)\\\\\n\\\\\n* * *\\\\\n\\\\\nExplore](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/tgv-inoui/destinations/lyon)\n\n* * *\n\n![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/image_scale_width_750/public/media/2026-10/option_master_750x490_en_0.jpg?itok=s_Pd1iFJ)\n\n![](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/image_scale_width_750/public/media/2026-10/option_master_750x490_en_0.jpg?itok=s_Pd1iFJ)\n\n## Hold your train ticket!\n\nNot ready to book? Secure your seat and fare for a limited time, with no immediate payment.\n\n[Discover](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/article/travelling-by-train-at-low-fares#ancre3)\n\nFind all our tips, advice, and useful information to help you  ahead and manage all your journeys.\n\n[Let's go](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/help/sncf-connect-evolves-with-you)\n\n## Looking for inspiration for your summer getaway?\n\n[![Halloween Londres : Big Ben soir](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_crop_460x345/public/media/2023-08/big-ben-soir.jpg?itok=umHyN5aI)\\\\\n\\\\\n**Halloween in London**\\\\\n\\\\\nThe clock is ticking: it’s almost time to put on your costumes and get ready to celebrate the scariest–and sweetest–night of the year! Why not spend Halloween in London this year?](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/article/halloween-in-london)\n\n[![Marché de Noël de Strasbourg](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_crop_460x345/public/media/2021-09/marche-de-noel-strasbourg.jpg?itok=ZKUVRiHO)\\\\\n\\\\\n**Christmas markets in Strasbourg**\\\\\n\\\\\nSince 1570, the Christmas market has settled in Strasbourg. It is the oldest Christmas market in Alsace and that is why Strasbourg has become the Christmas capital with its various markets and traditional events …](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/article/les-marches-de-noel-de-strasbourg)\n\n[![COUV- shopping noel à londres, regent street](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_crop_460x345/public/media/2022-09/shopping-noel-londres.jpg?itok=b34bLIxy)\\\\\n\\\\\n**Christmas in London**\\\\\n\\\\\nReady for some special Christmas shopping in London? Let’s go! Don’t miss the beautiful Christmas light displays on the busiest shopping streets in London: Regent Street, Oxford Street and Bond Street.](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/article/christmas-in-london)\n\n* * *\n\n[![bonhommes de neige marché](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/scale_crop_460x345/public/media/2019-09/istock-879145648.jpg?itok=su7TLZvU)\\\\\n\\\\\n**The opening of the Christmas sales on SNCF Connect**\\\\\n\\\\\nOn 30 September 2026, head to the SNCF Connect website or app for the opening of Christmas ticket sales. You’ll be able to book your upcoming journeys from 13 December 2026 up to 2 July 2027, depending on the destination and train operator.](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/article/ticket-sale-opening-on-sncf-connect)\n\n* * *\n\n[All our travel ideas](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/article)\n\n## You, your family, your luggage\n\n- [Our disability services](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/accessibility/preparation-trip-services)\n- * * *\n\n[For professionals](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/train/pro)\n- * * *\n\n[New luggage rules on board TGV Inoui trains](https://www.sncf-connect.com/en-en/help/transport-luggage)\n\n## Need help?\n\nAsk us your question..\n\nStart a conversationStart a conversation\n\nStart a conversationStart a conversation\n\n## One app to manage all your trips\n\nDownload the SNCF Connect app for faster, easier and cleaner mobility across France. With one touch, you can organise, book and manage your day-to-day trips as well as long-distance trips. Travel with peace of mind, and find all the information you need during your trip: e-tickets, real-time timetables, live traffic information...\n\n- [![Download on the app store](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/media/pushstore/en/apple-en.png) \\- New Tab](https://apps.apple.com/app/id343889987 \"Download on the app store - New Tab\")\n- [![Get it on google play](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/media/pushstore/en/google-en.png) \\- New Tab](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.vsct.vsc.mobile.horaireetresa.android&referrer=utm_source%3Dfooter%26utm_medium%3Dreferral%26utm_campaign%3Den-en&hl=en \"Get it on google play - New Tab\")\n\n![One app to manage all your trips](https://www.sncf-connect.com/assets/styles/image_scale_height_400/public/media/pushstore/2026-03/img_footer_en_0.png?itok=YOGfjkZE)\n\n* * *\n\n## Legal mentions\n\n### (1) All abroad for the Disney Halloween Festival!\n\n(1) **\\*Adult fares:** Price including VAT from €19 for a one-way journey with OUIGO, on selected days and trains, to a selection of more than 60 destinations in France and Belgium, subject to availability of seats at this fare on eligible trains. Offer valid for travel from 4 July to 12 December 2026 inclusive. Booking required no later than 10 minutes before train departure. Tickets are non-refundable and exchangeable subject to conditions. This offer cannot be combined with any other current promotion. Sales and information available on OUIGO.com, via the OUIGO app or from SNCF-approved online travel agencies.\n\n**List of destinations:** Aéroport Roissy Charles de Gaulle TGV ; Agde ; Agen ; Aix en Provence TGV ; Angers Saint-Laud ; Angoulême ; Antibes ; Aulnoye-Aymeries ; Auray ; Avignon TGV ; Béziers ; Bordeaux Saint-Jean ; Brest ; Bruxelles-Midi ; Cannes ; Creil ; Guingamp ; La Rochelle ; Laval ; Le Mans ; Les Arcs-Draguignan ; Lille ; Lorient ; Lyon (Lyon Perrache, Lyon Part Dieu, Lyon Saint Exupéry Aéroport) ; Marne-la-Vallée Chessy ; Marseille Saint-Charles ; Massy TGV ; Metz (Gare Lorraine TGV) ; Montauban ; Montpellier (Montpellier Saint-Roch, Montpellier Sud de France) ; Mons ; Morlaix ; Nantes ; Narbonne ; Nice ; Nîmes ; Niort ; Paris (Paris Est, Paris Gare de Lyon, Paris Gare du Nord, Paris Montparnasse) ; Perpignan ; Poitiers ; Quimper ; Rennes ; Saint-Raphaël Valescure ; Saint-Brieuc ; Saint-Quentin ; Sète ; Strasbourg ; Surgères ; TGV Haute Picardie ; Toulon ; Toulouse ; Tourcoing ; Tours (Gare St Pierre des Corps) ; Valence TGV Rhône Alpes Sud ; Vannes.\n\n### (2) Fancy Europe?\n\nPrices include VAT and start at the displayed rate per person for a one-way ticket in 2nd class, available on selected trains and dates, subject to availability. Offers are subject to the exchange and refund policies of the respective carriers, which can be viewed at the time of booking. Online payment only. Offers cannot be combined with other SNCF promotions.\n\n### (3) Lyon, where culture meets gastronomy\n\nPrices incl. VAT from, on a selection of destinations in France and Europe, and in 2nd class only. Available from 90 days before departure up to the time of train departure, subject to availability at this fare on eligible trains. Offer valid on TGV services (excluding OUIGO). Tickets can be exchanged and refunded subject to conditions. Offer cannot be combined with any other current promotion, advantage code or SNCF discounted fare (excluding SNCF Carte Avantage). Offer available in e-ticket format only.\n\nUntil 01/11 inclusive, all TGV INOUI tickets are 100% refundable and 100% exchangeable free of charge before departure (any fare difference between the original and the new ticket remains payable by you), regardless of the fare conditions of your tickets.\n\n* * *\n\n10276299.fls.doubleclick.net\n\n# 10276299.fls.doubleclick.net is blocked\n\nThis page has been blocked by an extension\n\n- Try disabling your extensions.\n\nERR\\_BLOCKED\\_BY\\_CLIENT\n\nReload\n\n\nThis page has been blocked by an extension\n\n![](<Base64-Image-Removed>)![](<Base64-Image-Removed>)",

        "url": "https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/travel-tips/getting-around-france-guide-transportation",
        "title": "Getting around France: A Complete Guide to Transportation | Travel Tips",
        "description": "Urban transport: Metro, tram, bus. Fares range from €1.50 to €2.50, Bordeaux: between €25 and €50 for a 2h30 journey. Bordeaux, a ticket costs €1.80. Fares ...",
        "position": 6,
        "markdown": "THIS SITE USES COOKIES\n\nWe use cookies to ensure the security and functionality of our sites. Some of these cookies are essential, while others help us to improve your experience. You can accept our recommended cookies by clicking \"Accept\" or click \"Modify\" to adjust your preferences.\n\nModify\n\nAccept\n\n[Blog](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog)\n>\n[Travel Tips](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/travel-tips)\n\n# Getting around France: A Complete Guide to Transportation\n\n![A couple cycling in Paris](https://uk.changegroup.com/dam/jcr:b13a1528-cc8c-42a5-97f2-90bd59f8430f/cycling-france.webp)\n\n**Table of contents:**\n\n1. [The Railway Network](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/travel-tips/getting-around-france-guide-transportation#The_Railway_Network)\n2. [The Highway Network](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/travel-tips/getting-around-france-guide-transportation#The_Highway_Network)\n3. [Getting Around the City](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/travel-tips/getting-around-france-guide-transportation#Getting_Around_the_City)\n\nKey takeaways:\n\n- French transport network: Very dense and diversified, with 866.1 billion passenger kilometres in 2021. The car is used by 84% of passengers, followed by rail (10%) and public transport (3%).\n- Rail: The SNCF network includes TGV, TER, Intercité and RER trains, with prices varying according to the route (Paris-Lyon between €25 and €45). It is advisable to book in advance to take advantage of the best deals.\n- Motorway network and car-sharing: France has 12,379 km of motorways, most of them under concession. Car-sharing via platforms such as Blablacar is an economical alternative for long-distance journeys.\n- Urban transport: Metro, tram, bus and self-service bicycle hire are popular options in major cities. Fares range from €1.50 to €2.50, and bikes can be free for 30 minutes.\n- Taxis and VTCs: Apps such as Uber, Bolt, and Lyft are common for quick journeys in cities such as Paris, Lyon, and Marseille. Prices vary according to distance; for example, a trip from Paris to Paris-CDG costs around €50-60.\n\n## The Railway Network\n\nWith 6.5 million journeys made each day by bus, tram, or metro, public transport is widely used in France.\n\nThe railway network, mainly operated by SNCF, is divided into several categories:\n\n- **TGV**: High-Speed Trains connecting major French cities such as Paris, Lyon, Bordeaux, Strasbourg, and Nantes.\n\n- **TER**: Regional Express Trains serving local areas.\n\n- **Intercités**: Trains connecting medium-sized towns and cities.\n\n- **RER**: Regional Express Network trains operating in the Île-de-France (Paris area).\n\n\nAverage SNCF ticket prices for trips from Paris are:\n\n- Paris – Bordeaux: between €25 and €50 for a 2h30 journey.\n\n- Paris – Lyon: between €25 and €45 for a 2-hour journey.\n\n- Paris – Lille: between €10 and €20 for a 1h10 journey.\n\n\nBe sure to book your tickets in advance to get the lowest prices. If you're staying in France for several months, consider getting a OUIGO subscription for discounts on train travel.\n\n## The Highway Network\n\nFrance has an extensive motorway network covering the entire country. As of 2023, it included 12,379 km of motorways, 75.5% of which operate under concession agreements. The main concessionaires managing and maintaining these motorways include Vinci Autoroutes, APRR, and SANEF.\n\nFrench people often prefer using their personal vehicles, and in 2021, an average of 28,000 vehicles used the highways daily, 84% of which were cars.\n\n### Carpooling\n\nSeveral carpooling platforms, such as [Blablacar](https://www.blablacar.fr/), allow you to travel long distances at lower costs. It's an ideal solution to save money and meet new people.\n\n### Car Rental\n\nIf you prefer full autonomy, you can rent a vehicle through one of the many rental companies in France.\n\nTo rent a car, you must:\n\n- Be at least 21 years old;\n\n- Have held a valid driver's license for at least 1–2 years;\n\n- Have a credit card in your name to pay the security deposit.\n\n\nThe average rental price for a small car ranges from €30 to €60. As with train tickets, booking in advance often gets you better rates. While basic insurance is included by default, you can opt for additional coverage for more peace of mind.\n\nIt’s usually possible to pick up and return the car in different cities, making this a flexible option for exploring the French countryside at your own pace.\n\n## Getting Around the City\n\nFor getting around in cities, consider using public transport or renting an electric bike to make your journeys easier.\n\n### Public Transport\n\nIn major cities like Paris, Lyon, Marseille, or Toulouse, you’ll find a wide variety of public transport options:\n\n- **Metro**: The fastest way to travel long distances within the city. A single metro ticket in Paris currently costs €2.50. In Lyon, it’s €2.10. In Toulouse and Bordeaux, a ticket costs €1.80.\n\n- **Tramway**: Most cities such as Dijon, Lyon, Nantes, or Bordeaux have their own tram networks. Fares generally range from €1.50 to €2.\n\n- **Bus**: Expect to pay between €1.50 and €2 for a bus ride, depending on the city. If you plan to use the bus multiple times, consider buying a 10-trip ticket for a discount.\n\n\n### Reserve your currency online\n\nEnjoy the best exchange rates and 0% commission when booking your travel money online.\n\n[Exchange Currency](https://tmouk.changegroup.com/uk_en/checkout)\n\n### Bike-Sharing Services\n\nThe most eco-friendly transportation option, self-service bikes are available in most major French cities, such as Vélib’ in Paris, Vélo’v in Lyon, and Bicloo in Nantes.\n\nPerfect for short trips or transfers, these bikes are often free for the first 30 minutes. A great way to explore the city!\n\n### Taxis and Ride-Hailing Services\n\nTaxi and ride-hailing apps are very popular in France, especially in larger cities. You can use Uber, Bolt, Lyft, or Heetch.\n\nCities where you can easily book a ride include:\n\n- Paris\n\n- [Marseille](https://fr.changegroup.com/nos-agences/bureaux-de-change-marseille)\n\n- Lyon\n\n- Bordeaux\n\n- Toulouse\n\n- Nice\n\n- Lille\n\n- Nantes\n\n- And more\n\n\nWhether you need a quick trip across Paris or a ride to [Toulouse Blagnac Airport](https://fr.changegroup.com/bureau-de-change-toulouse), this mode of transport is ideal for last-minute or late-night travel. Airports typically have designated pick-up zones for taxis and ride-hailing vehicles, so finding a driver is easy.\n\nIn smaller French towns, service availability may be more limited, but local taxi or VTC companies are usually accessible online.\n\nAs a general guide:\n\n- A trip from Charles de Gaulle Airport to Paris costs around €50–€60.\n\n- A ride from the Eiffel Tower to Gare de Lyon costs between €15 and €20.\n\n- In Toulouse, expect to pay about €25–€30 for a journey between Gare Matabiau and Toulouse Blagnac Airport.\n\n\nFrance's transportation network is highly versatile, offering a wide range of travel options. It continues to evolve every year, with ongoing improvements such as the expansion of cycling lanes.\n\nWhether you’re taking the TGV, hopping in a carpool, or cycling through [Paris](https://fr.changegroup.com/nos-agences/bureaux-de-change-paris), traveling around France will feel effortless. You now have everything you need to explore the country like a local!\n\n[![Travel Guide](https://uk.changegroup.com/dam/jcr:d091ad9d-0f2d-48ec-a293-0dc500db68f9/travel-guide.webp)](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-guides)\n\nRelated posts\n\n- [![4 Food Destinations to Explore in Thailand](https://uk.changegroup.com/.imaging/default/dam/change-group/BLOG/thai-food.webp/jcr:content.webp)](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/travel-inspiration/food-destinations-thailand)\n\n\n\n\n\n[4 Food Destinations to Explore in Thailand](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/travel-inspiration/food-destinations-thailand)\n\nJune 30, 2025\n\n- [![Transport in Asia](https://uk.changegroup.com/.imaging/default/dam/change-group/BLOG/transports-asia.webp/jcr:content.webp)](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/travel-tips/transports-asia)\n\n\n\n\n\n[Transport in Asia](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/travel-tips/transports-asia)\n\nJuly 21, 2025\n\n- [![Buying a property abroad](https://uk.changegroup.com/.imaging/default/dam/change-group/BLOG/comment-acheter-bien-immobilier-etranger.webp/jcr:content.webp)](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/business-travel/buy-property-abroad)\n\n\n\n\n\n[Buying a property abroad](https://uk.changegroup.com/travel-blog/business-travel/buy-property-abroad)\n\nApril 4, 2025",

journey time2hr 25m\n\nTrain operators5\n\nCraving a train journey from [Bordeaux](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/trains-to-bordeaux) to [Paris](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/trains-to-paris)? This guide will tell you everything you need to know to make the train ride from Bordeaux to Paris as delectable as a sip of the finest French wine.\n\n## Overview of train services\n\n### [SNCF (TGV INOUI)](https://www.raileurope.com/en/trains/tgv)\n\n#### Operator details\n\nSNCF is the national rail operator of France and a major player in European rail travel. Known for its reliable service, the company's expansive network takes connectivity to the next level, delivering smooth and seamless performance across all of France and nearby destinations. TGV INOUI, their premier high-speed rail service, operates the Bordeaux to Paris train.\n\n#### Route\n\nThe route departs from Bordeaux St-Jean train station and terminates at Paris Montparnasse train station. The train ride from Bordeaux to Paris often goes through some of France's most beautiful regions. The route may vary depending on the specific Bordeaux to Paris TGV train timetable. Some trains pass through [Tours](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/stations/tours), [Saint-Pierre-des-Corps](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/trains-to-saint-pierre-des-corps) or other notable locations. From here, you can push onward or take a breath in these hideaways.\n\n#### Train features\n\nTGV INOUI trains are the fastest trains from Bordeaux to [Paris](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/regions/paris), known for their speed and modern amenities. They typically include comfortable seating, free Wi-Fi, power outlets and onboard dining.\n\n#### Benefits\n\nThe [Bordeaux](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/regions/bordeaux-aquitaine) to Paris high-speed train is faster than driving, additionally saving you the expense and hassle of renting and returning a car, as well as fueling the car and dealing with traffic. The train goes directly to the city centre rather than a remote airport, simplifying onward travel to your hotel. By skipping traffic and security lines, you save time that would've been wasted on travelling by air or road.\n\n1st class train options from Bordeaux to [Paris](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/regions/paris-and-its-region) offer even more comfort, with spacious seating and enhanced services. Train travel is also the “greenest” form of travel, far below the carbon emissions of plane, auto or bus travel.\n\n## Schedule details\n\n### Quickest journey time and total distance\n\nThe fastest Bordeaux to Paris train time is approximately **2 hours and 3 minutes**, covering a total journey distance of roughly **500 kilometres**.\n\n### Earliest and latest departure times\n\nThe earliest train from Bordeaux to Paris departs on weekdays at approximately **05:18**, while the latest train departs at approximately **20:50**.\n\nOver the weekend, the earliest Bordeaux to Paris train departs at approximately **05:18**, while the latest train departs at approximately **22:12**.\n\n### Average number of daily trains\n\nApproximately **56 trains** operate daily from Bordeaux train station to Paris.\n\n### Frequency of departures\n\nA train from Bordeaux to Paris departs Bordeaux St-Jean station roughly every **13 minutes**.\n\n## Ticket information\n\n### Ticket prices\n\nBordeaux to Paris train costs vary by class, booking time and demand. Similar to airlines, prices fluctuate based on these factors. Use our booking widget to compare tickets and lock in savings. Know that peak travel periods, such as holidays and weekends, can impact pricing. Weekday travel might offer cheaper options if your schedule is flexible. Booking a direct Bordeaux to Paris fast train can sometimes be more expensive than connecting services.\n\nOur current live estimates of the Bordeaux to Paris train cost are as follows:\n\n- Cheapest ticket on the route: **US$12.00**\n\n- Average cost of a ticket on this route if you book 30 days in advance: **US$61**\n\n- Average cost of a ticket on this route if you book 7 days in advance: **US$81**\n\n\nTicket prices often depend on the season and booking time. Advance bookings usually offer better rates compared to last-minute purchases. Securing a spot well in advance is a smart move, especially when popular seasons are in full swing. This not only helps secure your preferred travel dates but also often results in lower fares. You may also have more seat selection options with an advance ticket.\n\nYou can book a Bordeaux to Paris train TGV ticket as far in advance as **12 months**.\n\n### Point-to-point tickets vs. rail passes\n\nThere are two major ways to board a high-speed train from Bordeaux to Paris — a point-to-point ticket or a rail pass.\n\n#### Point-to-point tickets\n\nPoint-to-point tickets are the simplest option for direct travel between Bordeaux and Paris. They reserve you a specific seat on a specific train itinerary departing at a specific time. You can easily book these tickets using our booking widget (on this page!). These are ideal for single journeys and offer a direct route without any stopovers — i.e., if there’s a stop along the way that you want to explore, you can’t just get off and then get back on another train.\n\n#### Rail passes you can use from Bordeaux to Paris\n\nRail passes are somewhat pricier but offer the freedom and flexibility of a true train adventure. With a pass, you get a set number of “train days” across a set number of months (this varies depending on the pass you choose). You don’t have to designate a specific train day — just pick any day within the pass period and jump on a train! Passes cover a number of different types of trains, giving you the option to enjoy the Bordeaux to Paris train route with spontaneous stops or changes to your itinerary.\n\nThe [Interrail Global Pass](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/passes/interrail-global-pass) or the [Eurail Global Pass](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/passes/eurail-global-passK) is an excellent choice for those seeking flexible travel across 33 countries. If you are considering staying an extended amount of time in France, consider the [France One Country Pass](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/passes/france-pass-eurail-interrail).\n\n## Train stations served\n\n### Bordeaux St-Jean\n\nSituated south of the Garonne River, the storied [Bordeaux St-Jean station](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/stations/bordeaux-st-jean) is a historic landmark where 19th-century flair merges with 20th-century panache in magnificent, harmonious architecture. First built in 1855, it was updated in 1898 with a metal train shed designed by none other than Gustave Eiffel, of tower fame. This sprawling station in Bordeaux serves as many as 18 million passengers every year.\n\n#### Facilities and services\n\n- Information desk\n\n- Ticket offices and machines\n\n- Luggage storage facilities\n\n- Public restrooms\n\n- Shops and newsstands\n\n- Restaurants and cafés\n\n- ATMs\n\n- Car rental services\n\n- Free Wi-Fi\n\n- Waiting areas\n\n- Taxi rank\n\n- Bus stops for local and airport routes\n\n- Accessibility services for passengers with reduced mobility\n\n\n### Paris Montparnasse\n\nOpened in 1840, [Paris Montparnasse station](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/stations/paris-gare-montparnasse) was extensively renovated in the 1960s into a staple of modernist architecture. Paris Montparnasse was the site of a famous train derailment in 1895, but the station has been almost entirely redesigned since then. The high-speed Bordeaux to Paris TGV trains pass through here and the 70 million passengers that use Montparnasse every year experience a safe, streamlined transit experience from end to end.\n\n#### Facilities and services\n\n- Information desk\n\n- Ticket offices and machines\n\n- Luggage storage facilities\n\n- Public restrooms\n\n- Shops and newsstands\n\n- Restaurants and cafés\n\n- ATMs\n\n- Car rental services\n\n- Free Wi-Fi\n\n- Waiting areas\n\n- Taxi rank\n\n- Bus stops for local and airport routes\n\n- Accessibility services for passengers with reduced mobility\n\n\n## Travelling with SNCF (TGV) – boarding process\n\nUnderstanding the boarding process for the Bordeaux to Paris TGV train can help make your journey smoother. From Bordeaux St-Jean, follow these steps:\n\n### Security and boarding procedures\n\nSecurity procedures, similar to airports, involve baggage scans. Tickets for the Bordeaux to Paris train can be validated physically, digitally on your smartphone or at a kiosk. The Paris arrival process by train is similar. Once through security, proceed to your designated platform and coach. This information is usually on your ticket.\n\n### Navigating the stations and boarding\n\nBoth Bordeaux St-Jean station and Paris Montparnasse station are designed for easy navigation. Clear signage guides passengers between platforms, amenities and connecting transportation. The stations handle substantial passenger traffic efficiently. If you have any questions, station agents are always nearby and ready to help.\n\n## Train classes of service\n\n### SNCF (TGV INOUI)\n\nThe TGV offers various classes, allowing you to choose the best fit. While initial discounts may exist, last-minute fares often become comparable to full price. Consider booking in advance for better pricing and availability.\n\n#### Second Class\n\nSNCF’s standard class provides comfortable seating and access to all onboard amenities. Booking ahead or scoring a deal can bring the Bordeaux to Paris train cost down to a level that's easy on the budget.\n\n#### First Class\n\nChoosing 1st class train service from Bordeaux to Paris affords you an extra helping of legroom and a reduced noise level, making for a far more peaceful and comfortable ride. You get more breathing space, cushier accommodations and top-shelf service with this enhanced package.\n\n## Travel documentation and customs requirements\n\nAlways check international travel requirements between your origin and destination countries. EU law governs travel within the European Union. Because the Bordeaux to Paris train ride never crosses any international border, documentation and customs requirements are even easier.\n\n### Documentation required\n\nAs both Bordeaux and Paris are within France and the EU, no specific documentation is needed beyond a valid train ticket. If your journey starts outside the EU, ensure you have the necessary travel documents for entering France and the EU — passport, visa if needed, etc.\n\n### Customs requirements\n\nSince the journey crosses no international borders, there is no customs check required for the high-speed train from Bordeaux to Paris. However, train personnel can ask to check your luggage and certain materials or merchandise (cash, tobacco, alcohol, etc.) may be prohibited in large quantities.\n\n## FAQs about the train journey from Bordeaux to Paris\n\nWant to know more about your journey from Bordeaux to Paris? We've compiled some of the most frequently asked questions by our customers to help you plan your journey.\n\n![](https://uploads.statics.raileurope.com/guides/cities/banner_images/default/1-4077206feaf4d19c44104459b5125ce2.jpg?1756800849)\n\nIs there a direct train from Bordeaux to Paris?\n\nYes, SNCF offers direct TGV trains from Bordeaux to Paris.\n\nIs there a direct train from Bordeaux to Paris airport?\n\nYes, TGV INOUI offers high-speed Bordeaux to Paris CDG train services to [Charles de Gaulle Airport](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/airports/paris-charles-de-gaulle). The train will drop you off on the first level of Terminal 2 of CDG, below the Sheraton Paris Airport &amp; Conference hotel.\n\nIs there a direct train from Bordeaux Airport to Paris?\n\nThere is no direct train from Bordeaux-Mérignac Airport (BOD) to Paris. To go directly from BOD to Paris, you will need to take ground transit from BOD to Bordeaux St-Jean train station and board an SNCF TGV train to Paris.\n\nWhich train station in Bordeaux goes to Paris?\n\nIn Bordeaux, the train station you will depart from to get to Paris is Bordeaux St-Jean station.\n\nHow long is the train journey from Bordeaux to Paris?\n\nThe Bordeaux to Paris train time is approximately **2 hours and 3 minutes** on a high-speed TGV train.\n\nHow far is Bordeaux to Paris by train?\n\nThe distance from Bordeaux to Paris by train is roughly **500 kilometres**.\n\nIs there a night train from Bordeaux to Paris?\n\nThere is no night train or sleeper train from Bordeaux to Paris at this time. The journey is too short to merit an overnight service. However, trains from Bordeaux to Paris depart all the way until **20:50**.\n\nCan you take a day trip from Bordeaux to Paris by train?\n\nIt is theoretically possible to take a day trip from Bordeaux to Paris. However, the train ride from Bordeaux to Paris is long enough that the round trip would take up most of the day … and this is Paris we’re talking about. You’d barely have enough time for a glass of wine and a croissant before it would be time to head back. Isn’t the City of Lights worth at least a few days? Consider staying awhile!\n\nIs the train from Bordeaux to Paris scenic?\n\nThe Bordeaux to Paris fast train offers a scenic experience. Starting in the famous wine region, the ride takes you past lush vineyards that make Bordeaux renowned worldwide. From there, the train traverses the French countryside, with rolling fields, forests and charming villages, before the urban landscape of Paris comes into view. It’s not breathtaking, but it’s definitely pleasant.\n\n[Paris to Bordeaux by train](https://www.raileurope.com/en/destinations/paris-bordeaux-train)\n\n## Other train journeys from Bordeaux\n\n- Whether you’re travelling solo, with friends, or as a couple, trains make it easy to plan a getaway that feels personal, flexible and entirely your own.Read more](https://www.raileurope.com/en/blog/love-optional-five-valentine-s-train-escapes-that-skip-the-cliches)\n- [![Europe awaits: cross-border train fares from just €39](https://uploads.statics.raileurope.com/blog/1264/thumb-5714658b87edcbe2943adfc7ddf84074.webp?1757069300)\\\\\n\\\\\n**Europe awaits: cross-border train fares from just €39** \\\\\n\\\\\nLooking for inspiration for your next European adventure? For one week only, you can score special cross-border fares starting at just €39 one-way.Read more](https://www.raileurope.com/en/blog/europe-awaits-cross-border-train-fares-from-just-39)\n- [![Where to feel like a local in France (even without speaking perfect French)](https://uploads.statics.raileurope.com/blog/1261/thumb-ba15874589d34ce70d1b28ab184f0993.webp?1756205008)\\\\\n\\\\\n**Where to feel like a local in France (even without speaking perfect French)** \\\\\n\\\\\nLet’s be honest. For many travellers, “living like a local” in France sounds like something reserved for fluent speakers, wine and cheese connoisseurs, or people with family there. But you don’t need any of that.Read more](https://www.raileurope.com/en/blog/where-to-feel-like-a-local-in-france-even-without-speaking-perfect-french)\n\nChild and youth passengers\n\nThe definition of \"Child\" and \"Youth\" varies by country and operator. This is why we ask for the age of young passengers.\n\nSometimes children below a certain age can travel without a seat for free. If you want to guarantee a seat for child passengers, enter '6' as the age of the child.\n\n[Read more about child and youth passenger ages.](https://help.raileurope.com/article/40848-child-youth-passenger-age-requirements) See also [youth discounts and railcards](https://help.raileurope.com/article/40858-youth-discounts-railcards).",

        "markdown": "Continue without agreeing →\n\n## ![Bonjour RATP](https://www.bonjour-ratp.fr/assets/site_logo_rectangle.webp)respects your privacy\n\nWith your agreement, we and our 27 partners use cookies or similar technologies to store, access, and process personal data like your visit on this website, IP addresses and cookie identifiers.
